Multiplicative update function:
def mult_step(a, loga, step, w, scale=1.0, clip=5.0):
  h = correlate_w_a(a,w)
  # h expected ~1/n, scale n
  h = h * len(a)
  h -= h.mean()
  # optional scale by std to make updates comparable
  std = h.std()
  if std < 1e-12: return a,loga,None
  # we can define update = -step * h / (max(1,std)?). If h mean-centered, std O(1) maybe. Use step as direct.
  u = -step * h
  u = np.clip(u, -clip, clip)
  new_loga = loga + u
  new_a = exp(new_loga - max)
  if sum zero return
  new_a /= sum
  return new_a,new_loga,h stats? 
But if h mean-centered but not std-normalized, step maybe 0.1. If h std 0.2, logs shift .02. Need maybe scale by 1/std to use step as fraction of one std: u = -step * (h - mean)/std. Then step 0.1 = 0.1 std. This adapts. Let's do u = -step * (h - h_mean) / max(std, 1e-6); clip. This may be too aggressive? For log domain, step 0.5 corresponds half std. We'll line search.

Implement simplified LP direction:
def lp_direction(a, M_cap, K, solver):
  ac=conv(a,a); Mmax=ac.max()
  M = min(Mmax, M_cap)
  Select top K indices where ac high? Or all if K<-1.
  Build matrix T shape (K,n): for each k in idx, T[row,j]=a[k-j] if 0≤k-j<n else 0.
  Variable g nonneg, no sum constraint? If maximize sum g with T@g≤M, sum g bounded if constraints cover all? If K small, may be unbounded (g in directions not constrained). Need add sum g ≤ current_sum? Since a sum=1, maximum sum can be n if uniform. To avoid unbounded, constrain sum g ≤ 2 maybe or n? Actually direction on simplex after scaling. We can constrain g ≥0, sum g =1? Then LP: maximize? If sum fixed, objective trivial. Need find vector in simplex satisfying f*g ≤ M. The old LP maximizes sum b with only nonneg and constraints, no sum fixed; to get a nonzero direction. But if unbounded due few constraints, it might blow. We should add u @ g <= 2 (or 1?) to bound. def quick_score(a):
  a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-9: return inf; a=a/s
  b=convolution(a); return 2.0*len(a)*b.max()

Let's implement weight functions robust:
def softmax_weights(c, T):
  T=max(T,1e-6)
  z=c/T
  if z.size==0: return zeros
  zmax=z.max()
  e=np.exp(z-zmax)
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0: w=np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
  else: w=e/s
  # ensure not too concentrated: maybe add floor: w=(1-alpha)w + alpha/len; alpha=1e-4
  w = 0.999*w + 1e-3/len? But if add uniform, changes scale; then normalize.
  return w
Penalty_weights(c, C, power=2, floor=True):
  v=np.maximum(0,c-C)
  w=v**power
  s=w.sum()
  if s<1e-15:
    # fallback softmax small T or top 50
    top=np.argsort(c)[-min(50,len(c)):]; w=zeros; w[top]=1
  else w/=s
  # maybe add tiny floor to all to maintain nonneg? weights only; gradient zero for nonviolating. Fine.
  return w
Top_weights(c, k=50, power=2): p = max(0, c - c.max()+threshold?) Use c - (max - 0.001) positive. Or take top k with weights c^power. If top all similar, ok.

But for penalty, if target C too low, no coefficients violate? If c.max <= C, then already feasible; lower C. We can use target C = min(bestMc - 1e-4, targetC). If no violation, we can set C = c.median? But if max <= target, we are done? We can still attempt lower target to surpass. Since target is upper bound, any below fine. But to maximize, lower. Function solvers selection:
_solver_name=None
def get_lp_solver():
  global _solver_name
  if not None return
  candidates = [cp.CLARABEL, cp.PDLP, cp.SCS, cp.ECOS]
  for s in candidates:
    try:
      x=cp.Variable(3, nonneg=True); prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x)==1]); prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False); if optimal: name=s; break
    except: pass
  if None: name=cp.SCS
  return

LP direction function:
def lp_direction(a, M_factor, K, solver):
  n=len(a); b=convolution_full(a); Mb=b.max(); if Mb<=0 return None
  M = Mb * M_factor
  # if K negative all
  if K >= 2*n-1: idx=np.arange(2*n-1)
  else: idx=np.argsort(b)[-K:]
  # To avoid T huge if K*n > 5e6, reduce K
  max_elems=4_000_000
  while len(idx)*n > max_elems and len(idx)>50: idx=idx[:len(idx)//2]
  Build T = np.zeros((len(idx), n), dtype=float)
  For each row, k: j_start=max(0,k-n+1); j_end=min(n,k+1); T[row, j_start:j_end] = a[k-j_start:k+1][::-1]? Need careful: T[row, j] = a[k-j]. For j from start to end-1, k-j from n-1 down? Let's fill vectorized:
     js = np.arange(j_start,j_end); T[row,js] = a[k-js]
  Loop rows Python K up to 500 okay.
  g=cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
  prob=cp.Problem(cp.Maximize(cp.sum(g)), [T@g <= M, cp.sum(g) <= 10.0])
  prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
  if status optimal/inaccurate: val=max(0,g.value); sumv=val.sum(); if sumv>0: return val/sumv else None
  else return None

Pseudo final:
"""
Minimize peak self-convolution of nonnegative sequence using multiplicative mirror descent with softmax/penalty focusing, Frank-Wolfe LP directions, random coordinate swaps, and multi-length interpolation.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
try: import cvxpy as cp
except: cp=None
try: from scipy.signal import correlate as _correlate  # maybe not used
except: _correlate=None

_global_solver=None

def get_solver(): ... using cp.

def next_fft_size(n):
    need=2*n-1
    if need<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
Check: need=5 -> (4).bit_length=3 ->8; need=3 ->2 bit_length2 ->4; need=1 ->0 bit_length0 ->1. Good. Actually 1 << (need-1).bit_length() for need=1 gives 1<<0=1. For need=2 n? n integer, need odd: 1,3,5. okay.

def convolution_full(a):
    n=len(a)
    L=next_fft_size(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]

def quick_score(a):
    a=np.ascontiguousarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf
    a=a/s
    b=convolution_full(a)
    # max over b, if b max slightly negative? no
    # Because of FFT, ensure central? maybe take max of b and 0
    return float(2.0*n*max(0.0,b.max()))

def norm_sum1(a): ...

def softmax_weights(c, T):
    T=max(float(T),1e-6)
    z=c/T
    zmax=z.max()
    # If z very small? ok
    e=np.exp(z - zmax)
    e_sum=e.sum()
    if e_sum<=1e-300:
       w=np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
    else:
       w=e/e_sum
    # add small floor
    floor=1e-4
    w=(1-floor)*w + floor*np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
    s=w.sum()
    return w/s

def penalty_weights(c, target_c, p=2.0):
    v=np.maximum(0.0, c - target_c)
    w=np.power(v, p)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
       # fallback top weight
       k=min(100,len(c))
       idx=np.argsort(c)[-k:]
       w=np.zeros_like(c)
       w[idx]=1.0
    s=w.sum()
    if s>0: w/=s
    return w

def correlate_weights(a,w):
    n=len(a); N=2*n-1; L=next_fft_size(n)
    Af=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    Wr=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    Wr[:N]=w[::-1]
    Wf=np.fft.rfft(Wr,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Af*Wf,L)
    # y_i = c[N-1-i] for i 0..n-1
    out=c[N-1:N-1-n:-1]
    return out

def apply_mult_update(a, loga, w, step, clip=5.0):
    n=len(a)
    out=correlate_weights(a,w)
    h=out * n
    h=h - h.mean()
    std=h.std()
    if std < 1e-12:
       return a, loga, None
    u = -step * h / std
    u = np.clip(u, -clip, clip)
    new_loga = loga + u
    # avoid huge differences by recenter
    m=new_loga.max()
    new_a=np.exp(new_loga - m)
    s=new_a.sum()
    if s<=1e-300:
       return a, loga, None
    new_a /= s
    return new_a, new_loga, h

def optimize_mult(a0, time_limit, target_score=1.50, start_T=0.4, end_T=0.05, mode='auto', seed=0):
   # mode: 'softmax','penalty','auto'
   a=norm_sum1(a0); n=len(a); t0=time.monotonic()
   best=a.copy(); best_score=quick_score(a); cur=a.copy(); cur_score=best_score; loga=np.log(np.maximum(cur,1e-15)); T=start_T; stall=0; iter=0; last_report=0
   # step sizes
   base_steps=[0.3,0.5,1.0,2.0]  # in std units after line search
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
      b=convolution_full(cur); c=b*n; cur_cmax=float(c.max()); cur_score=2*cur_cmax
      if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
         best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score; stall=0; last_improve=...
      if mode == 'penalty' or (mode=='auto' and best_score < min(target_score+0.03, 1.51)):
          target_c = target_score/2.0
          if target_c >= cur_cmax:
              target_c = cur_cmax - 1e-4
          w = penalty_weights(c, target_c)
      else:
          # adaptive T based on current distribution
          # For auto, T decreases over time from start_T to end_T
          elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
          progress=min(1.0, elapsed/max(1e-6,time_limit))
          T = start_T * (end_T/start_T) ** progress
          # Focus more on max by reducing T if stall
          if stall > 10:
             T = max(end_T*0.5, T*0.8)
          w=softmax_weights(c, T)
      selected=False
      # sort steps from small to large? Try small first to avoid overshoot.
      for step in [0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.2,2.0]:
          newa,newloga,_=apply_mult_update(cur,loga,w,step,clip=4.0)
          ns=quick_score(newa)
          if ns < cur_score - 1e-12:
              cur=newa; cur_score=ns; loga=newloga; selected=True; break
          # if it improves best but not cur? impossible ns < cur if cur maybe worse? We reset to best if cur worse.
      if selected:
         if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12: best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score; stall=0
         T = min(start_T, T*1.05)? maybe increase? no
      else:
         stall += 1
         if stall % 5 == 0:
             # perturb from best if stuck
             if stall >= 15:
                 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+stall)
                 noise = rng.normal(0, 0.02 + 0.005*stall, n)
                 pert = best * np.exp(noise)
                 pert = np.maximum(pert,0.0); s=pert.sum()
                 if s>1e-12:
                    cur=pert/s; cur_score=quick_score(cur); loga=np.log(np.maximum(cur,1e-15)); stall=0
                 else:
                    T=max(end_T*0.5, T*0.7)
             else:
                 T=max(end_T*0.5, T*0.7)
         # if no improvement for long, use top/penalty direct
         if stall > 60:
            # small random swap accepted if improves
            ... maybe coordinate step
            stall=20
      iter +=1
      if iter % 200 ==0 print
   return best,best_score

Implement interpolate_sequence:
def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
  old_n=len(f); if old_n==new_n: return f.copy()
  f=norm_sum1(f)
  x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
  x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
  g=np.interp(x_new,x_old,f)
  g=np.maximum(g,0)
  s=g.sum()
  if s<1e-12: g=np.ones(new_n)/new_n
  return g/s

Let's refine `optimize_proj`:
def optimize_proj(a0, time_limit, target_score, seed=0):
  a=norm_sum1(a0); best; current; t0
  steps_abs = [1e-7,2e-7,5e-7,1e-6,2e-6,5e-6,1e-5]
  T=0.2; stall=0
  while time<limit:

def coordinate_polish(a0,time,target_score,seed):
   a=norm; best; current; t0
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   no_improve=0
   while time:
      # compute weights to choose src/dst
      c=n*conv(a); if current close target: w=penalty_weights(c,target/2) else w=softmax_weights(c,0.1)
      out=correlate_weights(a,w); h=out*out? high gradient src reduce, low increase? We choose src high h, dst low h.
      h_norm=(h-h.min())/(h.max()-h.min()+eps)
      # top indices by h and mass
      mass_top=np.argsort(a * (1+h_norm))[-20:]
      low=np.argsort(a*(1-h_norm))[:20] maybe plus random low mass? dst should be low impact (not in high peaks)
      for src in mass_top:
         for dst in low:
            if src==dst: continue
            for frac in [0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05]:
               delta=frac*a[src]
               trial=a.copy(); trial[src]-=delta; trial[dst]+=delta; normalize; s=quick_score
               if s<current_score: accept; break all
      if no accept: random moves 10 times
      count no_improve; if no_improve large perturb.
  return best

  return best

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_mult(a0, time_limit, target_score=1.5, mode='auto', start_T=0.4, end_T=0.04, seed=0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    a=normalize_sequence(a0); n=len(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=quick_score(a)
    cur=best.copy(); cur_score=best_score
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(cur,1e-15))
    T=start_T; trial_T=end_T
    stall=0; it=0; mode2 = mode
    print(... initial?)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        b=convolution_full(cur); c=b*float(n); maxc=float(c.max())
        cur_score=2.0*maxc
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12 or it==0:
            best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score; stall=0
        if mode2=='penalty' or (mode2=='auto' and best_score < max(1.51, target_score+0.04)):
             target_c = target_score/2.0
             if target_c >= maxc:
                 target_c = maxc - 1e-4
             w=penalty_weights(c,target_c)
        else:
             w=softmax_weights(c,T)
        h = correlate_weights(cur,w)
        h = h * float(n)
        h -= h.mean()
        std = h.std()
        if std < 1e-12:
            # perturb
            ...
        selected=False
        # steps increasing but not too big
        for step in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,1.0,1.6):
             u = -step * h / std
             u = np.clip(u, -4.0, 4.0)
             new_loga = loga + u
             m = new_loga.max()
             new_a = np.exp(new_loga - m)
             s = new_a.sum()
             if s<=1e-300: continue
             new_a /= s
             ns=quick_score(new_a)
             if ns < cur_score - 1e-12:
                 cur=new_a; cur_score=ns; loga=new_loga; selected=True; break
        if selected:
             if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12: best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score; stall=0
             # focus a bit more
             T=max(end_T, min(start_T, T*0.97))
        else:
             stall += 1
             if stall <= 10:
                 T=max(end_T, T*0.85)
             elif stall <= 30:
                 T=max(end_T, T*0.7)
             else:
                 # reset/perturb from best
                 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+stall*100)
                 sigma=0.02+0.01*min(5,stall//10)
                 perturb = best*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
                 perturb=np.maximum(perturb,0.0)
                 s=perturb.sum()
                 if s>1e-12:
                     cur=perturb/s; cur_score=quick_score(cur); loga=np.log(np.maximum(cur,1e-15)); stall=0
                 else:
                     T=max(end_T, T*0.7)
        it += 1
        if it % 500 == 0:
             elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0; print(f"    mult it={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s score={best_score:.6f} T={T:.4f} stall={stall}")
    return best,best_score
```

If step line search finds improvement over cur but not over best? Since cur might be worse than best? We update best if cur_score<best; if cur==best always. If we ever perturb to worse, cur_score may be >best. Then line search may accept improvements to cur while cur_score still >best; best not updated until below best. Good. But `cur_score` initially best. If perturb worse, current is worse; `if cur_score < best_score` false. If an improvement from current still >best, we accept current drift, cur_score decreases but best unchanged. This can explore. Good.

def optimize_proj(a0,time_limit,target_score,seed=0):
  a=normalize_sequence(a0); best=a; best_score=quick_score(a); cur=best; cur_score=best_score; t0
  T=0.2; stall=0; it=0; steps=(1e-8,3e-8,1e-7,3e-7,1e-6,3e-6,1e-5)
  while time < limit:
    b=conv(cur); c=n*b
    if mode penalty: w=penalty_weights(c,target_score/2) else softmax(c,T)
    out=correlate(cur,w)
    d=out - out.mean()
    nrm=np.linalg.norm(d)
    if nrm<1e-15: stall++ continue/perturb
    d=d/nrm
    selected=False
    for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
      for eta in steps:
        trial=cur - sign*eta*d
        trial=project_simplex(trial)
        ns=quick_score(trial)
        if ns < cur_score - 1e-12: accept; selected=True; break
      if selected: break
    if selected: update best; T=max(end? maybe 0.05, T*0.98); stall=0
    else: stall++; T*=0.8; if stall>20 perturb
  return
```

Project simplex implementation from old:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv-1))[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1.0)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
Assumes sum target 1. If input sum not 1, project onto sum1. Good. If candidate sum maybe 1; after clip, sum adjust.
But if v has negative large, rho maybe? `np.nonzero(...)[0][-1]` could error if empty? For constant vector? For v all equal 1/n, condition? Let's test v all 1/n: sorted descending; u* k > sum_first_k -1 = k/n -1. For k<n, u*k=k/n > k/n -1 true; for k=n, 1 > 0 true. rho last n-1? n? good. No empty. Add fallback.

def coordinate_polish(a0,time_limit,target_score,seed=0):
 a=normalize; best; cur; cur_score; t0; rng=default_rng(seed); no_improve=0; T=0.15; while time:
   b=conv; c=n*b
   if best_score < target_score+0.02: w=penalty_weights(c,target_score/2) else w=softmax_weights(c,T)
   out=correlate; h=out; h_norm=(h-h.min())/(max-min+eps)
   # candidates src: high h and mass; dst: low h and lowish mass? We want move mass from contributing to peaks to less contributing.
   score_src = a*(1+h_norm)
   score_dst = a*(1-h_norm)
   src_list=np.argsort(score_src)[-30:]
   dst_list=np.argsort(score_dst)[:30]
   improved=False
   # Try a deterministic batch. Limit per iteration: maybe 200 moves.
   for src in src_list[:10]:
      if no_improve>5: break
      for dst in dst_list[:10]:
        if src==dst: continue
        for frac in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05):
          delta=frac*a[src]
          if delta<=0: continue
          trial=a.copy(); trial[src]-=delta; trial[dst]+=delta
          trial=normalize_sequence(trial)
          ns=quick_score(trial)
          if ns < cur_score - 1e-12: update; improved=True; break
        if improved: break
      if improved: break
   if not improved:
      # random moves 10
      for _ in range(10):
        i=rng.integers(n); j=rng.integers(n)
        if i==j: continue
        frac=rng.uniform(0.001,0.02)
        delta=frac*a[i]
        trial=a.copy(); ...
        if score improves update; else maybe accept small improvement to cur? same.
      no_improve +=1
      if no_improve>50: perturb
  return
```

`make_binomial_initial(n, gamma=0.0, beta=0.0)`:
```
a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0; rec=1.0
for k in range(1,n): rec *= (2*k-1)/(2.0*k); a[k]=rec
if gamma>0: a *= np.exp(-gamma*np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1))
if beta>0: x=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1); a *= (1-x)**beta
return normalize_sequence(a)
```

def prepare_mult_direction(a,w):
    out=correlate_weights(a,w)
    h=out*float(len(a))
    h -= h.mean()
    std=h.std()
    if std < 1e-12: return None,None
    return h, std

def apply_log_update(loga,h,std,step,clip=4.0):
    u=-step*h/std
    u=np.clip(u,-clip,clip)
    nl=loga+u
    m=nl.max()
    trial=np.exp(nl-m)
    s=trial.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return None
    return trial/s
```

    need = 2*n - 1
    if need <= 1:

    return 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / arr.size
    arr = arr / s
    return arr
```
If arr size 0, but quick_score returns inf. OK.

`quick_score`:
```
def quick_score(a):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float('inf')
    b = convolution_full(a)
    if b.size == 0: return inf
    m = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): return inf
    if m < 0: m=0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)
```

`convolution_full`:
```
def convolution_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = next_fft_size(n)
    Af = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(Af*Af, L)
    return b[:2*n-1]
```
For n=0, need? not.

`interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = normalize_sequence(f)
    old_n=f.size
    if new_n <=0: return f
    if old_n == new_n: return f.copy()
    if old_n ==0:
       return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    g = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f)
    g = np.maximum(g,0.0)
    s=g.sum()
    if s<1e-12: g=np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    return g/s
```
For old_n=1, linspace same? np.interp works. Good.

`make_binomial_initial`:
```
def make_binomial_initial(n, gamma=0.0, beta=0.0):
    if n <=0: return np.array([])
    a=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    reg=1.0; a[0]=1.0
    for k in range(1,n):
        reg *= (2.0*k - 1.0) / (2.0*k)
        a[k]=reg
    if gamma != 0.0 or beta !=0.0:
        x=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64) / max(1,n-1)
        if gamma != 0.0:
            a = a * np.exp(-abs(gamma)*x)  # gamma positive
        if beta != 0.0:
            a = a * np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - x) ** max(0.0, beta)
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```
If gamma negative? Use abs? We only pass positive. 

`make_invsqrt`:
```
def make_invsqrt_initial(n, gamma=0.0):
    if n<=0: return np.array([])
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    a = x ** (-0.5)
    if gamma != 0.0:
        t = np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)/max(1,n-1)
        a = a * np.exp(-abs(gamma)*t)
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```

`project_simplex`:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v.copy()
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rhs = cssv - 1.0
    idx = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n+1) > rhs)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        theta = (cssv[-1]-1.0)/float(n)
        out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    else:
        rho=idx[-1]
        theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
        out=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=1e-15:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    return out/s
```
The condition should use `u * np.arange(1, n+1) > cssv - 1` yes. If v sum not 1, project to sum1. For empty idx (shouldn't), theta=(sum-1)/n.

`softmax_weights`:
```
def softmax_weights(c, T):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    T = max(float(T), 1e-6)
    z = c / T
    zmax = float(z.max()) if z.size else 0.0
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones_like(c) / max(1, c.size)
    else:
        w = e / s
    floor = 1e-3
    w = (1.0 - floor)*w + floor*np.ones_like(c)/max(1,c.size)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: w=np.ones_like(c)/max(1,c.size)
    else: w/=s
    return w
```

`penalty_weights`:
```
def penalty_weights(c, target_c, power=2.0):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    v=np.maximum(0.0, c - float(target_c))
    w=np.power(v, power)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        # fallback top coefficients
        k = min(100, c.size)
        if k <= 0: return np.ones_like(c)
        idx=np.argsort(c)[-k:]
        w=np.zeros_like(c)
        vals=c[idx]
        if vals.max() > 0:
            w[idx]=vals
        else:
            w[idx]=1.0
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(c)/max(1,c.size)
    return w/s
```

`correlate_weights` as above. Need handle n=1.

`prepare_mult_direction`, `apply_log_update`:
```
def prepare_mult_direction(a,w):
    n=a.size
    out=correlate_weights(a,w)
    h=out*float(n)
    h=h - float(h.mean())
    std=float(h.std())
    if not np.isfinite(std) or std < 1e-14:
         return None, None
    return h, std

def apply_log_update(loga,h,std,step,clip=4.0):
    u=-step*h/std
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)): return None
    u=np.clip(u, -clip, clip)
    nl=loga+u
    m=float(nl.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m): return None
    trial=np.exp(nl-m)
    s=float(trial.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return None
    trial=trial/s
    return trial
```

`optimize_mult`: Need use `target_score` maybe None. In penalty target calculation, if target_score is None set 1.5. 
Add printing at start? Use no nested.
```
def optimize_mult(a0, time_limit, target_score=1.5, mode='auto', start_T=0.4, end_T=0.04, seed=0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a.size==0: return a, inf
    cur=a.copy(); best=a.copy(); cur_score=quick_score(a); best_score=cur_score
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(cur,1e-30))
    T=start_T; stall=0; it=0
    target = float(target_score) if np.isfinite(float(target_score)) else 1.5
    if time_limit <=0: return best,best_score
    min_T=max(0.005, end_T*0.5)
    print(f"    optimize_mult start n={a.size} score={best_score:.6f} mode={mode} target={target:.6f}")
    while True:
        elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit: break
        b=convolution_full(cur)
        c=b*float(cur.size)
        maxc=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        cur_score=2.0*maxc
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score; stall=0
        if mode == 'penalty' or (mode == 'auto' and best_score <= max(1.51, target + 0.05)):
            target_c=target/2.0
            if not np.isfinite(target_c) or target_c >= maxc:
                target_c = maxc - 1e-4
            if target_c < 0.0: target_c=0.0
            w=penalty_weights(c, target_c)
        else:
            w=softmax_weights(c, T)
        h,std=prepare_mult_direction(cur,w)
        selected=False
        if h is not None:
            for step in (0.08,0.15,0.3,0.55,0.9,1.4):
                new_a=apply_log_update(loga,h,std,step,clip=4.0)
                if new_a is None: continue
                ns=quick_score(new_a)
                if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
                    cur=new_a; cur_score=ns; loga=np.log(np.maximum(cur,1e-30)); selected=True; break
        if selected:
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score
            T=max(min_T, min(start_T, T*0.97))
            stall=0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall <= 10:
                T=max(min_T, T*0.85)
            elif stall <= 30:
                T=max(min_T, T*0.7)
            else:
                rng=np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000*stall)
                sigma=0.02 + 0.01*min(5, stall//10)
                pert=best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, cur.size))
                pert=np.maximum(pert, 0.0)
                s=float(pert.sum())
                if s>1e-12:
                    cur=pert/s
                    cur_score=quick_score(cur)
                    loga=np.log(np.maximum(cur,1e-30))
                    stall=0
                else:
                    T=max(min_T, T*0.7)
        it += 1
        if it % 1000 == 0 or (it % 200 == 0 and stall > 5):
            elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
            print(f"    mult it={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f} cur={cur_score:.6f} T={T:.4f} stall={stall}")
    return best, best_score
```
In line search, if current is worse than best, `cur_score` may be >best, but `if cur_score < best...` false, but if selected improves cur but still >=best, best unchanged. If eventually cur<best, update. Good. But in selected branch, we set stall=0 even if not improving best; okay.
Potential issue: If selected but cur_score not finite? not.

def optimize_proj(a0,time_limit,target_score=1.5,seed=0):
 t0=...

 while elapsed<limit:
   b=conv; c=n*b; maxc...
   update cur_score
   if best_score <= max(1.51,target+0.05): target_c=target/2; if target_c>=maxc: target_c=maxc-1e-4; w=penalty
   else: w=softmax(c,T)
   out=correlate(cur,w)
   d=out-out.mean()
   mabs=float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0
   if mabs<1e-15: stall++; continue/perturb
   d=d/mabs
   selected=False
   # Try negative (descent) first
   for eta in eta_list:
       trial=cur - eta*d
       trial=project_simplex(trial)
       ns=quick_score(trial)
       if ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
           cur=trial; cur_score=ns; selected=True; break
   if not selected:
       for eta in eta_list[:4]:
           trial=cur + eta*d
           trial=project_simplex(trial)
           ns=quick_score(trial)
           if ns < cur_score - 1e-13: accept; break
   if selected: update best; T=max(min_T,min(0.5,T*0.98)); stall=0
   else:
      stall++; T=max(min_T,T*0.85); if stall>20 perturb
   it++...
 return

`coordinate_polish`: Use time blocks. Let's implement simpler random plus gradient moves.
```
def coordinate_polish(a0,time_limit,target_score=1.5,seed=0):
 t0=...
 cur=norm; best; cur_score; T=0.1; no_imp=0; it=0; rng=default_rng
 while elapsed<limit:
   b=conv; c=n*b; maxc; cur_score=2maxc
   if best_score <= max(1.51,target+0.05): w=penalty(c,target/2 or maxc-1e-4)
   else: w=softmax(c,T)
   out=correlate(c? cur,w)
   h=out
   hmin,hmax; hnorm=(h-hmin)/(hmax-hmin+1e-15)
   src_score = cur*(1+hnorm)
   dst_score = cur*(1-hnorm)
   src_idx=np.argsort(src_score)[-20:]
   dst_idx=np.argsort(dst_score)[:20]
   improved=False
   # deterministic moves
   for src in src_idx[:8]:
      if improved or elapsed>limit: break
      for dst in dst_idx[:8]:
         if src==dst or improved: continue
         for frac in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02):
             delta=frac*cur[src]
             if delta <= 1e-15: continue
             trial=cur.copy()
             trial[src]-=delta; trial[dst]+=delta
             trial=normalize_sequence(trial)
             ns=quick_score(trial)
             if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
                 cur=trial; cur_score=ns; improved=True; break
         if improved: break
      no_imp = 0 if improved else no_imp+1
   if not improved:
      # random 8 moves
      for _ in range(8):
         if elapsed>limit: break
         i=int(rng.integers(cur.size)); j=int(rng.integers(cur.size))
         if i==j or cur[i] <=1e-12: continue
         frac=float(rng.uniform(0.001,0.02))
         delta=frac*cur[i]
         trial=cur.copy(); trial[i]-=delta; trial[j]+=delta
         trial=normalize_sequence(trial)
         ns=quick_score(trial)
         if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
             cur=trial; cur_score=ns; improved=True; break
   if improved:
      if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13: best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score
      no_imp=0
   else:
      no_imp +=1
      if no_imp > 40:
         rng2=default_rng(seed+it*101)
         sigma=0.02
         pert=best*np.exp(rng2.normal(0,sigma,cur.size)); pert=np.maximum(pert,0); s=pert.sum(); if s>0: cur=pert/s; cur_score=quick_score(cur); no_imp=10
   it +=1
   if it%100==0: print...
 return best,best_score
```
But `elapsed` variable needs update inside loops; use `time.monotonic()-t0`.
This can be slow but limited.

`get_lp_solver`: 
```
def get_lp_solver():
    global _lp_solver
    if _lp_solver is not None: return _lp_solver
    if cp is None: return None
    for s in [cp.CLARABEL, cp.PDLP, cp.SCS, cp.ECOS, cp.ECOS_BB]:
         try:
             x=cp.Variable(4, nonneg=True)
             prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x)==1, x<=0.5])
             prob.solve(solver=s, verbose=False)
             if prob.status in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate'):
                 _lp_solver=s; print; return s
         except Exception:
             continue
    _lp_solver=cp.SCS
    return cp.SCS
```

`lp_direction`:
```
def lp_direction(current, M_cap, K, solver):
    if solver is None: return None
    n=current.size; B=2*n-1
    if K >= B: idx=np.arange(B)
    else: idx=np.argsort(current_b?) Need b. We can compute b inside? To avoid recompute, pass b? But time. We'll compute b.
    b=convolution_full(current)
    Mb=float(b.max())
    if Mb<=0: return None
    M=M_cap if M_cap>0 else Mb
    # ensure K not too huge
    max_elems=3000000
    if K > 0 and (K*n) > max_elems:
        K = max(50, max_elems//n)
    idx=np.argsort(b)[::-1][:K]
    T=np.zeros((idx.size,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r,k in enumerate(idx):
       ki=int(k)
       js=max(0, ki-n+1); je=min(n, ki+1)
       if js < je:
           T[r, js:je] = current[ki-je+1:ki-js+1][::-1]
    g=cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
    prob=cp.Problem(cp.Maximize(cp.sum(g)), [T@g <= M, cp.sum(g) <= 4.0])
    try:
       prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if prob.status not in ('optimal','optimal_inaccurate'): return None
    val=np.asarray(g.value, dtype=np.float64)
    if val is None or val.shape != (n,): return None
    val=np.where(np.isfinite(val), val, 0.0)
    val=np.maximum(val,0.0)
    s=float(val.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12: return None
    return val/s
```
`T@g` with `T` numpy and g variable; okay. But if idx size 0? not.
Maybe add `M = M + 1e-15`.

def lp_frank_wolfe_phase(a0,time_limit,target_score=1.5,solver=None,K_init=250,K_max=900):
    if solver is None: return a0, quick_score(a0)
    t0=...
    cur=normalize; best; cur_score
    K=K_init; stall=0; it=0
    print...
    while time<limit:
       b=conv(current); Mb=b.max(); cur_score=2n*Mb
       target_b = target_score/(2n)
       if target_b > 0 and Mb > target_b*1.001:
           M = min(Mb*0.998, max(target_b*1.005, Mb*0.985))
       else:
           M = Mb*0.999
       g=lp_direction(cur,M,K,solver)
       if g is None:
          K=min(K+100,K_max)
          if K==K_max and stall>5: break
          stall+=1; continue
       best_s=cur_score; best_alpha=0; best_trial=None
       alphas=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0)
       for alpha in alphas:
          trial=np.asarray((1-alpha)*cur+alpha*g, dtype=np.float64)
          trial=normalize_sequence(trial)
          s=quick_score(trial)
          if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s - 1e-14:
              best_s=s; best_alpha=alpha; best_trial=trial
       if best_trial is not None:
          cur=best_trial; cur_score=best_s
          if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12: best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score
          stall=0
          K=max(200, K-20)
       else:
          stall +=1
          if stall % 3 == 0:
              K=min(K+100,K_max)
          if stall > 8:
              cur=best.copy()
              K=K_init
              stall=0
       it+=1
       if it%5==0: print...
    return best,best_score

Detailed code writing:

```
def evaluate_candidate(a):
    try:
        lst = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
```

for name in names:
    solver = getattr(cp, name, None)
    if solver is None: continue
    try:
       x=cp.Variable(4, nonneg=True)
       prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 0.5])
       prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
       if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
          _lp_solver = solver; print; return solver
    except Exception:
       continue
# fallback SCS if exists
solver = getattr(cp, "SCS", None)
if solver is not None: _lp_solver=solver; return solver
return None

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative step-function height sequence minimizing
2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

The implementation is a hybrid local search:
  - starts from the supplied parent when available, and also from
    binomial / inverse-square-root / tapered analytic initial vectors;
  - uses FFT-based exact convolution and exact self-convolution scores;
  - applies multiplicative mirror-descent updates driven by softmax or
    penalty weights placed on large convolution coefficients;
  - applies small projected-gradient moves;
  - applies randomized coordinate/swap polish;
  - optionally applies short Frank-Wolfe LP steps with cvxpy;
  - explores nearby lengths by interpolating the current best sequence.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

_lp_solver = None


def next_fft_size(n):
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if need <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()


def convolution_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = next_fft_size(n)
    af = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(af * af, L)
    return b[:2 * n - 1]


def normalize_sequence(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def quick_score(a):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    b = convolution_full(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def evaluate_candidate(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr]))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = normalize_sequence(f)
    old_n = f.size
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    g = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        g = np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    else:
        g = g / s
    return g


def make_binomial_initial(n, gamma=0.0, beta=0.0):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    rec = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        rec *= (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
        a[k] = rec
    if gamma != 0.0 or beta != 0.0:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        denom = max(1, n - 1)
        x = x / float(denom)
        if gamma != 0.0:
            a = a * np.exp(-abs(float(gamma)) * x)
        if beta != 0.0:
            a = a * np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - x) ** max(0.0, float(beta))
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def make_invsqrt_initial(n, gamma=0.0):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    a = x ** (-0.5)
    if gamma != 0.0:
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        denom = max(1, n - 1)
        t = t / float(denom)
        a = a * np.exp(-abs(float(gamma)) * t)
    return normalize_sequence(a)


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    rhs = cssv - 1.0
    idx = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > rhs)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        theta = (cssv[-1] - 1.0) / float(n)
    else:
        rho = idx[-1]
        theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out / s


def softmax_weights(c, T):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    T = max(float(T), 1e-6)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = c / T
    zmax = float(z.max()) if m else 0.0
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    else:
        w = e / s
    floor = 1e-3
    w = (1.0 - floor) * w + floor * (np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return w / s


def penalty_weights(c, target_c, power=2.0):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.maximum(0.0, c - float(target_c))
    w = np.power(v, float(power))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        k = min(120, m)
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        if k > 0:
            idx = np.argsort(c)[-k:]
            vals = c[idx]
            if float(vals.max()) > 0.0:
                w[idx] = vals
            else:
                w[idx] = 1.0
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return w / s


def correlate_weights(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(w[0]) * float(a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    N = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_fft_size(n)
    af = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    wr = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size >= N:
        wr[:N] = w[:N][::-1]
    else:
        tmp = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:w.size] = w
        wr[:N] = tmp[::-1]
    wf = np.fft.rfft(wr, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(af * wf, L)
    return c[N - 1:N - 1 - n:-1]


def prepare_mult_direction(a, w):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, None
    out = correlate_weights(a, w)
    h = out * float(n)
    h = h - float(h.mean())
    std = float(h.std())
    if not np.isfinite(std) or std < 1e-14:
        return None, None
    return h, std


def apply_log_update(loga, h, std, step, clip=4.0):
    u = -float(step) * h / std
    u = np.clip(u, -clip, clip)
    nl = loga + u
    m = float(np.max(nl))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return None
    trial = np.exp(nl - m)
    s = float(trial.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return trial / s


def optimize_mult(a0, time_limit, target_score=1.5, mode="auto",
                  start_T=0.4, end_T=0.04, seed=0):
    a0 = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a0.size == 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return a0, quick_score(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cur = a0.copy()
    best = a0.copy()
    cur_score = quick_score(cur)
    best_score = cur_score
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(cur, 1e-30))

    try:
        target = float(target_score)
    except Exception:
        target = 1.5
    if not np.isfinite(target):
        target = 1.5

    T = float(start_T)
    min_T = max(0.005, float(end_T) * 0.5)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    print(
        f"    optimize_mult start n={cur.size} score={best_score:.6f} "
        f"mode={mode} target={target:.6f}"
    )

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        b = convolution_full(cur)
        c = b * float(cur.size)
        maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        cur_score = 2.0 * maxc

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            stall = 0

        if mode == "penalty" or (mode == "auto" and best_score <= target + 0.001):
            target_c = target / 2.0
            if not np.isfinite(target_c) or target_c >= maxc:
                target_c = maxc - 1e-4
            if target_c < 0.0:
                target_c = 0.0
            w = penalty_weights(c, target_c)
        else:
            w = softmax_weights(c, T)

        h, std = prepare_mult_direction(cur, w)
        selected = False

        if h is not None:
            for step in (0.08, 0.15, 0.30, 0.55, 0.90, 1.40):
                new_a = apply_log_update(loga, h, std, step, clip=4.0)
                if new_a is None:
                    continue
                ns = quick_score(new_a)
                if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
                    cur = new_a
                    cur_score = ns
                    loga = np.log(np.maximum(cur, 1e-30))
                    selected = True
                    break

        if selected:
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = cur.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
            T = max(min_T, min(float(start_T), T * 0.97))
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall <= 10:
                T = max(min_T, T * 0.85)
            elif stall <= 30:
                T = max(min_T, T * 0.70)
            else:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000 * stall)
                sigma = 0.02 + 0.01 * min(5, stall // 10)
                pert = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, cur.size))
                pert = np.maximum(pert, 0.0)
                s = float(pert.sum())
                if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-12:
                    cur = pert / s
                    cur_score = quick_score(cur)
                    loga = np.log(np.maximum(cur, 1e-30))
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    T = max(min_T, T * 0.70)

        it += 1
        if it % 1000 == 0 or (it % 200 == 0 and stall > 5):
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(
                f"    mult it={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f} "
                f"cur={cur_score:.6f} T={T:.4f} stall={stall}"
            )

    return best, best_score


def optimize_proj(a0, time_limit, target_score=1.5, seed=0):
    a0 = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a0.size == 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return a0, quick_score(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cur = a0.copy()
    best = a0.copy()
    cur_score = quick_score(cur)
    best_score = cur_score

    try:
        target = float(target_score)
    except Exception:
        target = 1.5
    if not np.isfinite(target):
        target = 1.5

    T = 0.25
    min_T = 0.02
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    eta_list = (1e-8, 2e-8, 5e-8, 1e-7, 2e-7, 5e-7, 1e-6, 2e-6)

    print(
        f"    optimize_proj start n={cur.size} score={best_score:.6f} "
        f"target={target:.6f}"
    )

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        b = convolution_full(cur)
        c = b * float(cur.size)
        maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        cur_score = 2.0 * maxc

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            stall = 0

        if best_score <= target + 0.001:
            target_c = target / 2.0
            if not np.isfinite(target_c) or target_c >= maxc:
                target_c = maxc - 1e-4
            if target_c < 0.0:
                target_c = 0.0
            w = penalty_weights(c, target_c)
        else:
            w = softmax_weights(c, T)

        out = correlate_weights(cur, w)
        d = out - float(out.mean())
        mabs = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0
        selected = False

        if np.isfinite(mabs) and mabs > 1e-15:
            d = d / mabs
            for eta in eta_list:
                trial = cur - eta * d
                trial = project_simplex(trial)
                ns = quick_score(trial)
                if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
                    cur = trial
                    cur_score = ns
                    selected = True
                    break
            if not selected:
                for eta in eta_list[:4]:
                    trial = cur + eta * d
                    trial = project_simplex(trial)
                    ns = quick_score(trial)
                    if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
                        cur = trial
                        cur_score = ns
                        selected = True
                        break

        if selected:
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = cur.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
            T = max(min_T, min(0.5, T * 0.98))
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            T = max(min_T, T * 0.85)
            if stall > 20:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 3000 * stall + 1)
                pert = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.025, cur.size))
                pert = np.maximum(pert, 0.0)
                s = float(pert.sum())
                if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-12:
                    cur = pert / s
                    cur_score = quick_score(cur)
                    stall = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 500 == 0:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(
                f"    proj it={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f} "
                f"cur={cur_score:.6f} T={T:.4f} stall={stall}"
            )

    return best, best_score


def coordinate_polish(a0, time_limit, target_score=1.5, seed=0):
    a0 = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a0.size == 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return a0, quick_score(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cur = a0.copy()
    best = a0.copy()
    cur_score = quick_score(cur)
    best_score = cur_score
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        target = float(target_score)
    except Exception:
        target = 1.5
    if not np.isfinite(target):
        target = 1.5

    T = 0.12
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    print(
        f"    coordinate_polish start n={cur.size} score={best_score:.6f} "
        f"target={target:.6f}"
    )

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        b = convolution_full(cur)
        c = b * float(cur.size)
        maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        cur_score = 2.0 * maxc
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            no_improve = 0

        if best_score <= target + 0.001:
            target_c = target / 2.0
            if not np.isfinite(target_c) or target_c >= maxc:
                target_c = maxc - 1e-4
            if target_c < 0.0:
                target_c = 0.0
            w = penalty_weights(c, target_c)
        else:
            w = softmax_weights(c, T)

        out = correlate_weights(cur, w)
        o = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
        omin = float(o.min())
        omax = float(o.max())
        span = omax - omin
        if not np.isfinite(span) or span < 1e-15:
            hnorm = np.zeros(cur.size, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            hnorm = (o - omin) / span

        src_score = cur * (1.0 + hnorm)
        dst_score = cur * (1.0 - hnorm)

        src_idx = np.argsort(src_score)[-16:]
        dst_idx = np.argsort(dst_score)[:16]

        improved = False

        for src in src_idx[:8]:
            if improved or time.monotonic() - t0 >= time_limit:
                break
            src = int(src)
            if cur[src] <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for dst in dst_idx[:8]:
                dst = int(dst)
                if dst == src or improved:
                    continue
                for frac in (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02):
                    delta = frac * cur[src]
                    if delta <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    trial = cur.copy()
                    trial[src] -= delta
                    trial[dst] += delta
                    trial = normalize_sequence(trial)
                    ns = quick_score(trial)
                    if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
                        cur = trial
                        cur_score = ns
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

        if not improved:
            for _ in range(8):
                if time.monotonic() - t0 >= time_limit:
                    break
                i = int(rng.integers(cur.size))
                j = int(rng.integers(cur.size))
                if i == j or cur[i] <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.001, 0.02))
                delta = frac * cur[i]
                trial = cur.copy()
                trial[i] -= delta
                trial[j] += delta
                trial = normalize_sequence(trial)
                ns = quick_score(trial)
                if np.isfinite(ns) and ns < cur_score - 1e-13:
                    cur = trial
                    cur_score = ns
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = cur.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 40:
                rng2 = np.random.default_rng(seed + 9000 * it + 7)
                pert = best * np.exp(rng2.normal(0.0, 0.022, cur.size))
                pert = np.maximum(pert, 0.0)
                s = float(pert.sum())
                if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-12:
                    cur = pert / s
                    cur_score = quick_score(cur)
                    no_improve = 10

        it += 1
        if it % 100 == 0:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(
                f"    coord it={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f} "
                f"cur={cur_score:.6f} no_improve={no_improve}"
            )

    return best, best_score


def get_lp_solver():
    global _lp_solver
    if _lp_solver is not None:
        return _lp_solver
    if cp is None:
        return None

    names = ["CLARABEL", "PDLP", "SCS", "ECOS"]
    for name in names:
        solver = getattr(cp, name, None)
        if solver is None:
            continue
        try:
            x = cp.Variable(4, nonneg=True)
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 0.5])
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                _lp_solver = solver
                print(f"    LP solver selected: {name}")
                return _lp_solver
        except Exception:
            continue

    solver = getattr(cp, "SCS", None)
    if solver is not None:
        _lp_solver = solver
        print("    LP solver fallback: SCS")
        return _lp_solver

    return None


def lp_direction(current, M_cap, K, solver):
    if solver is None:
        return None
    current = np.asarray(current, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = current.size
    if n == 0:
        return None

    b = convolution_full(current)
    Mb = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(Mb) or Mb <= 0.0:
        return None

    M = float(M_cap)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        M = Mb

    B = 2 * n - 1
    if K >= B:
        idx = np.arange(B)
    else:
        idx = np.argsort(b)[::-1][:max(1, int(K))]

    max_elems = 4_000_000
    if idx.size * n > max_elems and idx.size > 50:
        idx = idx[:max(50, max_elems // n)]

    Tmat = np.zeros((idx.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, kk in enumerate(idx):
        ki = int(kk)
        js = max(0, ki - n + 1)
        je = min(n, ki + 1)
        if js < je:
            Tmat[r, js:je] = current[ki - je + 1:ki - js + 1][::-1]

    g = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
    try:
        prob = cp.Problem(
            cp.Maximize(cp.sum(g)),
            [Tmat @ g <= M, cp.sum(g) <= 4.0]
        )
        prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
    except Exception:
        return None

    if prob.status not in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
        return None

    val = getattr(g, "value", None)
    if val is None:
        return None
    val = np.asarray(val, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if val.size != n:
        return None
    val = np.where(np.isfinite(val), val, 0.0)
    val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
    s = float(val.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return val / s


def lp_frank_wolfe_phase(a0, time_limit, target_score=1.5,
                         solver=None, K_init=300, K_max=1000):
    a0 = normalize_sequence(a0)
    if a0.size == 0 or time_limit <= 0 or solver is None:
        return a0, quick_score(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cur = a0.copy()
    best = a0.copy()
    cur_score = quick_score(cur)
    best_score = cur_score

    try:
        target = float(target_score)
    except Exception:
        target = 1.5
    if not np.isfinite(target):
        target = 1.5

    K = int(K_init)
    Kmax = int(K_max)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    print(
        f"    lp_frank_wolfe start n={cur.size} score={best_score:.6f} "
        f"target={target:.6f} K={K}"
    )

    while True:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed >= time_limit:
            break

        n = cur.size
        b = convolution_full(cur)
        Mb = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
        cur_score = 2.0 * Mb

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            stall = 0

        target_b = target / (2.0 * n) if n > 0 else Mb
        if np.isfinite(target_b) and target_b > 0 and Mb > target_b * 1.001:
            M = min(Mb * 0.998, max(target_b * 1.005, Mb * 0.985))
        else:
            M = Mb * 0.999

        g = lp_direction(cur, M, K, solver)
        if g is None:
            K = min(K + 100, Kmax)
            stall += 1
            if K == Kmax and stall > 6:
                break
            continue

        best_s = cur_score
        best_alpha = 0.0
        best_trial = None

        for alpha in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 1.0):
            trial = (1.0 - alpha) * cur + alpha * g
            trial = normalize_sequence(trial)
            s = quick_score(trial)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s - 1e-14:
                best_s = s
                best_alpha = alpha
                best_trial = trial

        if best_trial is not None:
            cur = best_trial
            cur_score = best_s
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = cur.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
            stall = 0
            K = max(200, K - 20)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 3 == 0:
                K = min(K + 100, Kmax)
            if stall > 8:
                cur = best.copy()
                cur_score = best_score
                K = int(K_init)
                stall = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 5 == 0:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
            print(
                f"    lp it={it} t={elapsed:.0f}s best={best_score:.6f} "
                f"cur={cur_score:.6f} K={K} stall={stall}"
            )

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate():
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    TOTAL = 1770.0

    print("propose_candidate: start")

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if p.sum() > 1e-9:
                parent = normalize_sequence(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = make_binomial_initial(6200)

    best = parent.copy()
    best_score = quick_score(best)
    print(f"initial length={best.size} score={best_score:.6f}")

    variants = []
    for gamma in (0.0, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0):
        variants.append(make_binomial_initial(best.size, gamma=gamma))
    for gamma in (0.0, 0.3, 0.7):
        variants.append(make_invsqrt_initial(best.size, gamma=gamma))
    for delta in (-400, 400, -800, 800):
        nn = best.size + delta
        if 2000 <= nn <= 12000:
            variants.append(interpolate_sequence(best, nn))

    for cand in variants:
        if cand is None or cand.size == 0:
            continue
        sc = quick_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            print(f"quick better variant n={best.size} score={best_score:.6f}")

    rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t1 = min(rem * 0.45, 820.0)
    if t1 > 40:
        best, best_score = optimize_mult(
            best, t1, target_score=1.503, mode="auto",
            start_T=0.5, end_T=0.05, seed=17
        )

    rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t2 = min(rem * 0.22, 350.0)
    if t2 > 25:
        target2 = max(1.5005, best_score - 0.0015)
        best, best_score = optimize_mult(
            best, t2, target_score=target2, mode="penalty",
            start_T=0.16, end_T=0.025, seed=37
        )

    rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t3 = min(rem * 0.10, 150.0)
    if t3 > 15:
        target3 = max(1.4985, best_score - 0.0012)
        best, best_score = optimize_proj(best, t3, target3, seed=57)

    rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t4 = min(rem * 0.08, 110.0)
    if t4 > 10:
        target4 = max(1.4975, best_score - 0.0012)
        best, best_score = coordinate_polish(best, t4, target4, seed=77)

    rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t5 = min(rem * 0.30, 300.0)
    if t5 > 60 and best.size <= 8000:
        solver = get_lp_solver()
        if solver is not None:
            target5 = max(1.4970, best_score - 0.0015)
            best, best_score = lp_frank_wolfe_phase(
                best, t5, target5, solver, K_init=320, K_max=1100
            )

    rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t6 = min(rem * 0.35, 250.0)
    if t6 > 40:
        target6 = max(1.4960, best_score - 0.0020)
        best, best_score = optimize_mult(
            best, t6, target_score=target6, mode="auto",
            start_T=0.35, end_T=0.02, seed=97
        )

    rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    t7 = min(rem * 0.35, 90.0)
    if t7 > 10:
        target7 = max(1.4950, best_score - 0.0010)
        best, best_score = coordinate_polish(best, t7, target7, seed=117)

    rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 150:
        trial_lengths = []
        for delta in (-500, 500, -1000, 1000):
            nn = best.size + delta
            if 2500 <= nn <= 10000:
                trial_lengths.append(nn)
        trial_lengths = list(dict.fromkeys(trial_lengths))

        for nn in trial_lengths[:2]:
            rem = TOTAL - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            if rem < 90:
                break
            cand = interpolate_sequence(best, nn)
            sc = quick_score(cand)
            if not (np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score + 0.012):
                continue
            tt = min(rem * 0.45, 140.0)
            if tt < 20:
                continue
            target_t = max(1.4955, best_score - 0.0012)
            cand, sc = optimize_mult(
                cand, tt, target_score=target_t, mode="auto",
                start_T=0.4, end_T=0.03, seed=137 + nn % 1000
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = cand
                best_score = sc
                print(f"length variant improved n={best.size} score={best_score:.6f}")

    out = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out = np.minimum(out, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        out += (0.01 - s) / float(out.size)
    elif s > 0.0:
        out = out / s

    qscore = quick_score(out)
    escore = evaluate_candidate(out)
    print(
        f"FINAL n={out.size} quick={qscore:.6f} evaluate={escore:.6f} "
        f"time={time.monotonic() - t_global:.0f}s"
    )

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
